  318 | 	// Removes the applied option, if one is applied, so the next option starts from the full list.
  319 | 	// "Applied" means the option shows as selected, or Amazon's Clear control is showing (a click that only partly worked).
  320 | 	// If clearing does not work, the page is reset to the unfiltered listing, so the next option always starts clean.
  321 | 	private async clearAppliedOption(filterName: string, filterValue: string): Promise<void> {
  322 | 		// Let a page load that is still in flight finish, so the checks below see the page as it ends up.
  323 | 		await this.page.waitForLoadState("domcontentloaded").catch(() => {});
  324 | 
  325 | 		const selectedOption: Locator = this.productPage.getAppliedFilterByTypeAndName(filterName, filterValue).first();
  326 | 		const clearControl: Locator = this.productPage.clearButtonBy.first();
  327 | 
  328 | 		const optionSelected: boolean = await selectedOption.isVisible();
  329 | 		let clearShown: boolean = await clearControl.isVisible();
  330 | 		if (!optionSelected && !clearShown) {
  331 | 			log.info(`'${filterValue}' is not applied; nothing to clear`);
  332 | 			return;
  333 | 		}
  334 | 
  335 | 		// 1. The option is selected, so Amazon should be showing Clear too. Give it a moment before deciding it is missing.
  336 | 		if (optionSelected && !clearShown) {
  337 | 			clearShown = await clearControl.waitFor({ state: "visible", timeout: 5_000 }).then(() => true).catch(() => false);
  338 | 		}
  339 | 
  340 | 		// 2. Click Clear, then confirm the option is no longer selected.
  341 | 		let cleared: boolean = false;
  342 | 		if (clearShown) {
  343 | 			try {
  344 | 				await this.genericUtility.clearBlockers();
  345 | 				await clearControl.click();
  346 | 				await this.genericUtility.clearBlockers();
  347 | 				await selectedOption.waitFor({ state: "hidden", timeout: 10_000 });
  348 | 				cleared = true;
  349 | 			} catch {
  350 | 				cleared = false;
  351 | 			}
  352 | 		}
  353 | 
  354 | 		if (cleared) {
  355 | 			log.info(`'${filterValue}' cleared; it no longer shows as selected`);
  356 | 			return;
  357 | 		}
  358 | 
  359 | 		// 3. Anything else: reset the page, so the next option does not get added on top of this one.
  360 | 		log.warn(`'${filterValue}': could not clear it (selected: ${optionSelected}, Clear showing: ${clearShown}); going back to the unfiltered listing instead`);
  361 | 		await this.goBackToUnfilteredListing();
  362 | 	}
  363 | 
  364 | 	// Resets the page to the unfiltered listing (listingUrl), so the next option starts clean.
  365 | 	private async goBackToUnfilteredListing(): Promise<void> {
  366 | 		await this.page.goto(this.listingUrl);
  367 | 		await this.genericUtility.clearBlockers();
  368 | 		log.info(`Back on the unfiltered listing (${this.genericUtility.shortUrl(this.page.url())})`);

  371 | 
  372 | 	// ---- Helpers: the products listed under an applied option ----
  373 | 
  374 | 	// The results refresh gradually after an option is applied. Waits (at most 10s) until the number of product titles is
  375 | 	// the same twice, 0.5s apart, and returns it. Returns the last count seen (0 when none showed). Never throws.
  376 | 	private async waitForProductsToSettle(filterValue: string): Promise<number> {
  377 | 		const deadline: number = Date.now() + 10_000;
  378 | 		let lastCount: number = -1;
  379 | 		while (Date.now() < deadline) {
  380 | 			const count: number = await this.productPage.productNameListingPageBy.count();
  381 | 			if (count > 0 && count === lastCount) {
  382 | 				return count;
  383 | 			}
  384 | 			lastCount = count;
  385 | 			await this.page.waitForTimeout(500);
  386 | 		}
  387 | 		log.warn(`'${filterValue}': ${lastCount > 0 ? `the number of products was still changing after 10s (last count ${lastCount})` : "no product title appeared within 10s"}`);
  388 | 		return Math.max(lastCount, 0);
  389 | 	}
  390 | 
  391 | 	// How many of the listed products to read for the applied option. RunConfig sets the limit. Products open a page each,
  392 | 	// so they have a small limit; cards are read in the listing and have their own, higher limit.
  393 | 	private async productsToRead(filterValue: string, kind: "products" | "cards" = "products"): Promise<number> {
  394 | 		const listed: number = await this.waitForProductsToSettle(filterValue);
  395 | 		const toRead: number = kind === "cards" ? RunConfig.discountCardLimit(listed) : RunConfig.productLimit(listed);
  396 | 		log.info(`'${filterValue}': ${listed} product(s) listed, reading ${toRead} ${kind === "cards" ? "card(s) (no page is opened)" : "product page(s)"}`);
  397 | 		if (listed === 0) {
  398 | 			log.warn(`'${filterValue}': the listing is empty after applying the option, so nothing to read (${await this.where()})`);
  399 | 		}
  400 | 		return toRead;
  401 | 	}

  410 | 	// Reads one product's card in the listing: its title and the discount printed on it, for example "(44% off)" -> 44.
  411 | 	// Returns null, with a warning, when the card cannot be read.
  412 | 	private async readDiscountCard(productIndex: number, filterValue: string): Promise<DiscountCard | null> {
  413 | 		const title: Locator = this.productPage.getProductByIndex(productIndex);
  414 | 		try {
  415 | 			// The title and the card text are read in one browser call, so both come from the same moment and the same
  416 | 			// product. The card is the closest result container around the title. A short timeout: the title is already there.
  417 | 			const read = await title.evaluate(
  418 | 				(titleElement) => ({
  419 | 					title: (titleElement as HTMLElement).innerText,
  420 | 					card: (titleElement.closest("div[data-component-type='s-search-result']") as HTMLElement | null)?.innerText ?? null,
  421 | 				}),
  422 | 				undefined,
  423 | 				{ timeout: 5_000 },
  424 | 			);
  425 | 			if (read.card === null) {
  426 | 				log.warn(`'${filterValue}' card ${productIndex + 1}: no result card around the title '${read.title.trim().slice(0, 60)}', so it is skipped`);
  427 | 				return null;
  428 | 			}
  429 | 			const titleText: string = read.title.trim();
  430 | 			const cardText: string = read.card.replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  431 | 
  432 | 			// The discount is printed as "(44% off)". A card can also carry other "% off" text (bank offers, coupons), so
  433 | 			// look for the printed discount first. Without it, a single "% off" text is still safe to use.
  434 | 			const printedDiscount = cardText.match(/\((\d+)% off\)/);
  435 | 			const mentions: string[] = cardText.match(/\d+% off/g) ?? [];
  436 | 			let discount: number | null = null;
  437 | 			let source: string = "no discount shown";
  438 | 			if (printedDiscount) {
  439 | 				discount = Number(printedDiscount[1]);
  440 | 				source = `printed as "${printedDiscount[0]}"`;
  441 | 			} else if (mentions.length === 1) {
  442 | 				discount = Number(mentions[0].replace(/\D.*$/, ""));
  443 | 				source = `the only "% off" text on the card, "${mentions[0]}"`;
  444 | 			}
  445 | 			if (mentions.length > 1) {
  446 | 				log.warn(`'${filterValue}' card ${productIndex + 1}: ${mentions.length} "% off" texts on the card (${mentions.join(", ")}); ${printedDiscount ? "used the printed discount" : "none is the printed discount, so no discount was taken"}`);
  447 | 			}
  448 | 
  449 | 			log.info(`'${filterValue}' card ${productIndex + 1}: ${discount === null ? "no discount shown" : discount + "% off (" + source + ")"}, title '${titleText.slice(0, 60)}'`);
  450 | 			return { filter: filterValue, title: titleText, discount, cardText };
  451 | 		} catch (error) {
  452 | 			log.warn(`'${filterValue}' card ${productIndex + 1}: could not be read: ${firstLine(error)}`);
  453 | 			return null;
  454 | 		}
  455 | 	}

  458 | 	// ---- Helpers: reading one product (open its page in a new tab, read the details, close the tab) ----
  459 | 
  460 | 	// Opens the product in a new tab and reads its details. Always returns a map with the same keys: a product that
  461 | 	// could not be opened or loaded keeps empty values and loaded = false. The tab is always closed.
  462 | 	private async readProductPage(productIndex: number, filterValue: string): Promise<ProductResult> {
  463 | 		const label: string = `'${filterValue}' product ${productIndex + 1}`;
  464 | 
  465 | 		const productResult: ProductResult = new Map<string, ProductValue>([
  466 | 			["filter", filterValue],
  467 | 			["title", null],
  468 | 			["keyFeatures", null],
  469 | 			["about", null],
  470 | 			["techDetails", null],
  471 | 			["loaded", false],
  472 | 		]);
  473 | 
  474 | 		const productTab: Page | undefined = await this.openProductTab(productIndex, filterValue);
  475 | 		if (!productTab) {
  476 | 			log.warn(`${label}: no product tab opened, so its details are skipped`);
  477 | 			return productResult;
  478 | 		}
  479 | 
  480 | 		try {
  481 | 			// Locators belong to the page they were created for, so the product tab needs its own page objects.
  482 | 			const detailPage = new ProductListingPage(productTab);
  483 | 			const detailUtility = new GenericUtility(productTab, this.testInfo);
  484 | 
  485 | 			if (await this.waitForProductPageToLoad(productTab, detailPage, detailUtility, label, filterValue, productIndex)) {
  486 | 				productResult.set("loaded", true);
  487 | 				await this.readProductDetails(productTab, detailPage, detailUtility, productResult, label, filterValue, productIndex);
  488 | 			}
  489 | 		} finally {
  490 | 			// Whatever happened above, close the product tab, so the listing tab is the only one left for the next product.
  491 | 			await productTab.close().catch(() => {});
  492 | 			log.info(`${label}: product tab closed`);
  493 | 		}
  494 | 
  495 | 		log.info(`${label}: ${productResult.get("loaded") ? "READ" : "NOT read"}, text found for: ${this.fieldsRead(productResult)}`);
  496 | 		return productResult;
  497 | 	}
  498 | 
  499 | 	// Which of the four text fields of a product result have text.
  500 | 	private fieldsRead(productResult: ProductResult): string {
  501 | 		const found: string[] = ["title", "keyFeatures", "about", "techDetails"].filter((key) => productResult.get(key) !== null);
  502 | 		return found.join(", ") || "nothing";
  503 | 	}
